\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{j_{t},I_{t+1}}\left[\frac{1}{\alpha\cdot d_{j_{t},t+1}}+\frac{(\tilde{q}^{*}_{j_{t},t}-\hat{q}^{*}_{j_{t},t})^{2}}{\alpha}\mid\mathcal{G}\right]\cdot P(\mathcal{G}).

Our proof is thus completed.

\square

Appendix F
Missing Proofs for
Section

4

Proof of
Lemma

4.3
.

We first show that it is optimal to restrict the range of the dual variable
\tilde{\bm{\mu}}
of the relaxed offline optimum
\bar{V}^{\text{Off}}_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})
(
10
) into the region
\Omega=\{\bm{\mu}\geq 0:\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}\leq 2\}
.

The dual problem of
\bar{V}^{\text{Off}}_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})
(
10
) can be written as follows

\min_{\bm{\mu}\geq 0}\sum_{i\in[m]}\mu_{i}\cdot c_{i}+\sum_{\tau=t}^{T}\max_{x_{\tau}\in[0,1]}\left\{\tilde{r}_{\tau}\cdot x_{\tau}-\sum_{i\in[m]}\mu_{i}\cdot\tilde{a}_{\tau,i}\cdot x_{\tau}\right\}.

We prove that any optimal solution to the above dual problem must belong to the region
\Omega=\{\bm{\mu}\geq 0:\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}\leq 2\}
by showing contradiction. Suppose that there is a dual variable
\hat{\bm{\mu}}
optimal to the dual problem above and it holds that
\hat{\mu}_{1}+\hat{\mu}_{2}+\hat{\mu}_{3}>2
. Clearly, all type 4 queries will be rejected. We classify it into two scenarios.

Scenario 1: suppose that there exists a type
i=1,2,3
, say type
i=1
, that will not be reject, i.e.,
\hat{\mu}_{2}+\hat{\mu}_{3}\leq 1
. Then, we know that
\hat{\mu}_{1}>1
, which implies that type
2
and type
3
queries will all be rejected. Note that
\hat{\mu}_{2}+\hat{\mu}_{3}\leq 1
implies
\hat{\mu}_{1}-1\geq\hat{\mu}_{1}+\hat{\mu}_{2}+\hat{\mu}_{3}-2
.
We can obtain a new dual variable that
\hat{\mu}_{2}^{\prime}=\hat{\mu}_{2}
,
\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{3}=\hat{\mu}_{3}
, and
\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{1}=\hat{\mu}_{1}-\delta
, for a positive constant
\delta=\hat{\mu}_{1}+\hat{\mu}_{2}+\hat{\mu}_{3}-2
satisfying
\delta\leq\hat{\mu}_{1}-1
. It is clear to see that
\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{1}+\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{2}+\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{3}\geq 2
and
\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{1}\geq 1
, and thus the primal solution and
\hat{\bm{\mu}}^{\prime}
still satisfy the saddle-point condition and thus
\hat{\bm{\mu}}^{\prime}
is optimal. Therefore, we know that it is optimal to select
\hat{\bm{\mu}}^{\prime}
as the optimal dual variable and it holds that
\hat{\bm{\mu}}^{\prime}\in\Omega
.

Scenario 2: suppose that it holds
\hat{\mu}_{1}+\hat{\mu}_{2}>1
,
\hat{\mu}_{2}+\hat{\mu}_{3}>1
, and
\hat{\mu}_{1}+\hat{\mu}_{3}>1
. Then we set
\delta^{\prime}=\min\{\hat{\mu}_{1}+\hat{\mu}_{2},\hat{\mu}_{1}+\hat{\mu}_{3},\hat{\mu}_{2}+\hat{\mu}_{3},\frac{\hat{\mu}_{1}+\hat{\mu}_{2}+\hat{\mu}_{3}}{2}\}
and we obtain a new dual variable
\hat{\bm{\mu}}^{\prime}=\delta^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\mu}
. It is clear to see that
\hat{\bm{\mu}}^{\prime}
satisfies
\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{1}+\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{2}\geq 1
,
\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{2}+\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{3}\geq 1
,
\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{1}+\hat{\mu}^{\prime}_{3}\geq 1
and
\hat{\mu}_{1}^{\prime}+\hat{\mu}_{2}^{\prime}+\hat{\mu}_{3}^{\prime}\geq 2
. Thus the primal solution and
\hat{\bm{\mu}}^{\prime}
still satisfy the saddle-point condition and thus
\hat{\bm{\mu}}^{\prime}
is optimal. Now
\hat{\bm{\mu}}^{\prime}
either satisfies the condition in scenario 1 or
\hat{\bm{\mu}}^{\prime}\in\Omega
. In either case, we can finally show that it is optimal to restrict the range of the dual variable to the region
\Omega=\{\bm{\mu}\geq 0:\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}\leq 2\}
.

We now show that
Section

4
is satisfied. For any
\bm{\mu}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime}\in\Omega
, we know that

F(\bm{a}_{4}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}|\bm{a}_{4})-F(\bm{a}_{4}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime}|\bm{a}_{4})=\frac{1}{2}\cdot(\bm{a}_{4}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}-\bm{a}_{4}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime}).

Therefore, it holds that

\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}\left[\left(F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}|\tilde{\bm{a}})-F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime}|\tilde{\bm{a}})\right)\cdot(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime})\right]\geq\frac{1}{2}\cdot(\bm{a}_{4}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}-\bm{a}_{4}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\geq\frac{p_{4}}{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}\left[(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\right]

where the last inequality holds by noting that

(\bm{a}_{4}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}-\bm{a}_{4}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime})^{2}=\|\bm{\mu}^{\prime}-\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime}\|_{2}^{2}\geq(\bm{a}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}-\bm{a}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime})^{2}

for all
i=1,2,3
. On the other hand, it is clear to see that

\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}\left[\left(F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}|\tilde{\bm{a}})-F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime}|\tilde{\bm{a}})\right)\cdot(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}\left[(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime\prime})^{2}\right]

since the densities are all upper bound by 1. Therefore, we know that
Section

4
is satisfied.

We finally show that the strict complementary slackness condition is violated by (
25
). Denote by
\mu_{i}
the dual variable for constraint for resource
i
. Then, it is clear to see that the primal-dual pair
\mu_{1}^{*}=\mu^{*}_{3}=\frac{1-\varepsilon}{2}
,
\mu^{*}_{2}=0
and
q^{*}_{1}=q^{*}_{3}=q^{*}_{4}=\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}
,
q^{*}_{2}=\varepsilon
is optimal to (
25
) by checking that the saddle point condition is satisfied, for any
\varepsilon>0
. To be specific, it holds that

\displaystyle\mu^{*}_{1}\cdot\left(p_{2}q^{*}_{2}+p_{3}q^{*}_{3}+p_{4}q^{*}_{4}-\frac{5\varepsilon(1+\varepsilon)}{2(1+6\varepsilon)}\right)=0

\displaystyle\mu^{*}_{2}\cdot\left(p_{1}q^{*}_{1}+p_{3}q^{*}_{3}+p_{4}q^{*}_{4}-\frac{5\varepsilon(1+\varepsilon)}{2(1+6\varepsilon)}\right)=0

\displaystyle\mu^{*}_{3}\cdot\left(p_{1}q^{*}_{1}+p_{2}q^{*}_{2}+p_{4}q^{*}_{4}-\frac{5\varepsilon(1+\varepsilon)}{2(1+6\varepsilon)}\right)=0

and

\displaystyle q^{*}_{1}=1-\mu^{*}_{2}-\mu^{*}_{3},\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ q^{*}_{2}=1-\mu^{*}_{1}-\mu^{*}_{3}

\displaystyle q^{*}_{3}=1-\mu^{*}_{1}-\mu^{*}_{2},\penalty\ \penalty\ \penalty\ q^{*}_{4}=\frac{1}{2}\cdot(2-\mu^{*}_{1}-\mu^{*}_{2}-\mu^{*}_{3}).

Therefore, the saddle point conditions are satisfied and the primal-dual pair
\mu_{1}^{*}=\mu^{*}_{3}=\frac{1-\varepsilon}{2}
,
\mu^{*}_{2}=0
and
q^{*}_{1}=q^{*}_{3}=q^{*}_{4}=\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}
,
q^{*}_{2}=\varepsilon
is optimal to (
25
).
However, while the resource constraint is binding for every
i=1,2,3
, the optimal dual variable
\mu^{*}_{2}=0
, which shows that the strict complementary slackness condition is not satisfied for every
\varepsilon>0
. Our proof is thus completed.

\square

Proof of
Lemma

4.4
.

By plugging (
27
) into (
8
), we have that

\displaystyle\text{Myopic}_{t}(\pi,\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t})}\left[\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}[\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t}}(I_{t})]-\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t}-\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}\cdot\tilde{x}^{\pi}_{t}}(I_{t+1})]]-\tilde{r}_{t}\cdot\tilde{x}^{\pi}_{t}\right]

(97)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t})}\left[\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}[(\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}-\tilde{x}^{\pi}_{t})\cdot(\tilde{r}_{t}+\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}_{t}^{\pi}-\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1}))+(\tilde{x}^{\pi}_{t}-\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t})\cdot\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}_{t}^{\pi}}(I_{t+1})+G_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t}}(I_{t})]\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t})}[(\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}-\tilde{x}^{\pi}_{t})\cdot(\tilde{r}_{t}-M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}_{t}^{\pi},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1}))]\right]+\mathbb{E}[G_{\tilde{\bm{c}}_{t}^{\pi}}(I_{t})].

From the definition of
\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}
and
\tilde{x}^{\pi}_{t}
, we know that
\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}-\tilde{x}^{\pi}_{t}\in\{-1,1\}
if and only if
\hat{M}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\leq\tilde{r}_{t}\leq M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})
and
\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}
, or
M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})\leq\tilde{r}_{t}\leq\hat{M}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}
and
\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}
. Thus, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t})}[(\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}-\tilde{x}^{\pi}_{t})\cdot(\tilde{r}_{t}-M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}_{t}^{\pi},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1}))]\right]

(98)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{r}_{t}\sim F(\cdot|\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t})}[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\hat{M}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\leq\tilde{r}_{t}\leq M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})\}}\cdot(M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})-\hat{M}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}})]\right]\right]\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}\}}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{r}_{t}\sim F(\cdot|\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t})}[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\hat{M}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\geq\tilde{r}_{t}\geq M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})\}}\cdot(\hat{M}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}-M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1}))]\right]\right]\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}\}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bar{\alpha}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[(\hat{M}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}-M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1}))^{2}\right]\right]\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}\}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\bar{\alpha}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}\}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[(\hat{M}_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}-\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}[M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})])^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+2\bar{\alpha}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}\}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}\left[(\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}[M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})]-M_{\tilde{\bm{c}}^{\pi}_{t},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1}))^{2}\right]\right]

where the second inequality follows from
Section

4
which implies that the density of
\tilde{r}_{t}
is upper bounded by
\bar{\alpha}
and the third inequality follows from
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
for any
a,b
. Plugging (
98
) into (
97
), we complete our proof of the lemma.

\square

Proof of
Lemma

4.6
.

Note that we have

L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu})=\frac{\bm{c}^{\top}\bm{\mu}}{s-1}+\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{r}\sim F_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}}[\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}]^{+}|\tilde{\bm{a}}\right]=\frac{\bm{c}^{\top}\bm{\mu}}{s-1}+\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}\left[\int_{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}}^{\infty}(\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu})f(r|\tilde{\bm{a}})dr\right]

which implies that

\frac{\partial L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu})}{\partial\mu_{i}}=\frac{c_{i}}{s-1}-\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[\tilde{a}_{i}\cdot(1-F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}|\tilde{\bm{a}}))],\penalty\ \penalty\ \forall i\in[m]

and

\frac{\partial^{2}L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu})}{\partial\mu_{i}\partial\mu_{i^{\prime}}}=\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[f(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}|\tilde{\bm{a}})\cdot\tilde{a}_{i}\cdot\tilde{a}_{i^{\prime}}],\penalty\ \penalty\ \forall i,i^{\prime}\in[m]

where we use
\tilde{a}_{i}
to denote the
i
-th element of vector
\tilde{\bm{a}}
.
Moreover, note that for any
\bm{\mu}\geq 0
, from Taylor’s theorem, there exists a
\bm{\mu}^{\prime}
that lies on the intersection between
\bm{\mu}
and
\bm{\mu}^{*}
such that

L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu})-L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c}}(\mu^{*})=(\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*})^{\top}\frac{\partial L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu}^{*})}{\partial\bm{\mu}}+\frac{1}{2}\cdot(\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*})^{\top}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu}^{\prime})}{\partial\bm{\mu}^{2}}\cdot(\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*})

where
\frac{\partial L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu}^{*})}{\partial\bm{\mu}}=(\frac{\partial L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu}^{*})}{\partial\mu_{1}},\ldots,\frac{\partial L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu}^{*})}{\partial\mu_{m}})
, and
\frac{\partial^{2}L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu}^{\prime})}{\partial\bm{\mu}^{2}}
is the Hessian matrix that equals
\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[f(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}|\tilde{\bm{a}})\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}]
. Note that
\Omega
is assumed to be a convex set in
Section

4
and both
\bm{\mu},\bm{\mu}^{*}\in\Omega
. Then, we have
\bm{\mu}^{\prime}\in\Omega
and
Section

4
implies that
\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[f(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{\prime}|\tilde{\bm{a}})]\geq\underline{\alpha}
. The smallest positive eigenvalue of
\frac{\partial^{2}L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu}^{\prime})}{\partial\bm{\mu}^{2}}
is therefore lower bounded by
\underline{\alpha}\cdot\underline{\beta}
. Also, from the optimality of
\bm{\mu}^{*}
, we must have

(\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*})^{\top}\frac{\partial L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu}^{*})}{\partial\bm{\mu}}\geq 0

for any
\bm{\mu}\geq 0
. Thus, it holds that

\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{2}\cdot\|\mathcal{P}_{S}(\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\cdot(\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*})^{\top}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu}^{\prime})}{\partial\bm{\mu}^{2}}\cdot(\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*})\leq L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu})-L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu}^{*})

which completes our proof.

\square

Proof of
Lemma

4.7
.

From (
33
), we know that

\displaystyle M_{\bm{c},\bm{a}_{t}}(I_{t+1})-\mathbb{E}[M_{\bm{c},\bm{a}_{t}}(I_{t+1})]\leq\bm{a}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{a}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}],

\displaystyle\text{if\penalty\ }M_{\bm{c},\bm{a}_{t}}(I_{t+1})\geq\mathbb{E}[M_{\bm{c},\bm{a}_{t}}(I_{t+1})]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[M_{\bm{c},\bm{a}_{t}}(I_{t+1})]-M_{\bm{c},\bm{a}_{t}}(I_{t+1})\leq\mathbb{E}[\bm{a}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}]-\bm{a}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1},

\displaystyle\text{if\penalty\ }M_{\bm{c},\bm{a}_{t}}(I_{t+1})\leq\mathbb{E}[M_{\bm{c},\bm{a}_{t}}(I_{t+1})]

which implies that

\mathrm{Var}(M_{\bm{c},\bm{a}_{t}}(I_{t+1}))\leq 2\mathrm{Var}(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})+2\mathrm{Var}(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2})+2(\mathbb{E}[\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}]-\mathbb{E}[\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}])^{2}.

Note that we have

\mathrm{Var}(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})=\mathbb{E}[(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}+\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}])^{2}]\leq 2\mathbb{E}[(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}]+2(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}])^{2}.

Similarly, we have

\mathrm{Var}(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2})=\mathbb{E}[(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}+\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}])^{2}]\leq 2\mathbb{E}[(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})^{2}]+2(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}])^{2}.

Also, we have

\displaystyle(\mathbb{E}[\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}]-\mathbb{E}[\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}])^{2}

\displaystyle=(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}]-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}+\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}+\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}])^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}]-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})^{2}+3(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}+3(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}])^{2}.

Thus, we have that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(M_{\bm{c},\bm{a}_{t}}(I_{t+1}))

\displaystyle\leq 10(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}]-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}+10(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}]-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})^{2}+6(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}

(99)

\displaystyle\penalty\penalty\penalty\displaystyle+4\mathbb{E}[(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}]+4\mathbb{E}[(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 14\mathbb{E}[(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}]+14\mathbb{E}[(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})^{2}]+6(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}.

It only remains to bound the term
(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}
. We have the following claim, where the proof is relegated to the end of this proof.

Claim 4

It holds that

(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}))^{2}\leq\frac{2}{(s-1)\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\cdot\bar{d}^{3}\cdot m^{1/2}\cdot\gamma

where
\bar{d}=\max_{j\in[n]}\{\|\bm{a}_{j}\|_{2}\}
and
\gamma=\max_{i\in[m],j\in[n]:a_{j,i}>0}\frac{u_{j}}{a_{j,i}}
.

Therefore, our proof is completed by combining (
99
) and
4
.

\square

Proof of
4
.

From
Lemma

4.6
, by substituting
\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}
into
\bm{\mu}^{*}
, and substituting
\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}
into
\bm{\mu}
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{2}\cdot\|\mathcal{P}_{S}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})-L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})=L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})-L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c}-\bm{a}_{t},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})+L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c}-\bm{a}_{t},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})-L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})

\displaystyle\leq L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})-L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c}-\bm{a}_{t},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})=\frac{1}{s-1}\cdot\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}

where the second inequality follows from
L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c}-\bm{a}_{t},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})\leq L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c}-\bm{a}_{t},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})\leq L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})
by noting that
\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}\in\argmin_{\bm{\mu}\geq 0}L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c}-\bm{a}_{t},t+1}(\bm{\mu})
.
Thus, we have

(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}))^{2}\leq\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\|\mathcal{P}_{S}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})\|^{2}_{2}\leq\frac{2}{(s-1)\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\cdot(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})\cdot\bar{d}^{2}\leq\frac{2}{(s-1)\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\cdot\|\bm{a}_{t}\|_{2}\cdot\|\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}\|_{2}\cdot\bar{d}^{2}.

Clearly, we must have
\hat{\mu}_{2,i}\leq\gamma
for each
i\in[m]
with
\gamma=\max_{i\in[m],j\in[n]:a_{j,i}>0}\frac{u_{j}}{a_{j,i}}
, which implies that
\|\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{m}\gamma
. Thus, it holds that

(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}))^{2}\leq\frac{2}{(s-1)\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\cdot\bar{d}^{3}\cdot m^{1/2}\cdot\gamma

which completes our proof.

\square

Proof of
Lemma

4.8
.

We first consider the setting where
\bm{c}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}
and thus both
\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}
and
\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}
are well-defined in (
32
).
We show that
\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}\neq\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}
happens only if
\bm{a}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}\leq\tilde{r}_{t}\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}
. Note that the value of
\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}
can be determined in the following way:

\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}=\argmax_{\phi\in[0,1]}\phi\cdot\tilde{r}_{t}+\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}-\phi\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})-\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}}(I_{t+1}).

(100)

Note that for any
\phi\in[0,1]
, we must have

-\phi\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}\leq\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}-\phi\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})-\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}}(I_{t+1})\leq-\phi\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}

which can be proved following the same intuition of
Lemma

4.5
.
Therefore, when
\tilde{r}_{t}\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}
, we must have
\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}=\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}=0
, and when
\tilde{r}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}
, we must have
\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}=\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}=1
. We conclude that
G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})=0
when
\tilde{r}_{t}\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}
or
\tilde{r}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}
. We now assume that
\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}\leq\tilde{r}_{t}\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}
and we further consider two cases as follows:

Case 1: If
\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}=0
, then we have

G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})=\tilde{r}_{t}\cdot\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}+\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}-\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}\cdot\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}}(I_{t+1})-\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}}(I_{t+1}).

Note that we have

\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}-\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}\cdot\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}}(I_{t+1})-\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}}(I_{t+1})\leq-\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}.

By noting
\tilde{r}_{t}\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}
and
\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}\leq 1
, we have that

G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})\leq\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}\cdot(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}

which gives us an upper bound for the first case.

Case 2: If
\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}=1
, then we have

G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})=\tilde{r}_{t}\cdot(\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}-1)+\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}-\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}\cdot\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}}(I_{t+1})-\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}-\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1}).

Note that we have

\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}-\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}\cdot\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}}(I_{t+1})-\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}-\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})\leq(1-\tilde{x}^{*}_{t})\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}.

By noting
\tilde{r}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}
and
0\leq 1-\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}\leq 1
, we have that

G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})\leq(1-\tilde{x}^{*}_{t})\cdot(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}

which gives us an upper bound for the second case. Therefore, on both cases, we show that if
\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}\leq\tilde{r}_{t}\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}
, it holds that

G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}.

(101)

which implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{r}_{t}\sim F(\cdot|\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t})}[G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})]\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{r}_{t}\sim F(\cdot|\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t})}[\mathbbm{1}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}\leq\tilde{r}_{t}\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}}\cdot(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})]\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\alpha}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}\left[(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}\right]\right].

We further note that

\displaystyle(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}

\displaystyle=(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}+\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}+\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})^{2}+3(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}+3(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}.

We use
4
to bound the term
(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}
. Then, we have

\mathbb{E}_{I_{t}}\left[G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})\right]\leq\frac{6\bar{\alpha}}{(s-1)\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\bar{d}^{3}m^{1/2}\gamma+3\bar{\alpha}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}[(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}]\right]+3\bar{\alpha}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}[(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{2})^{2}]\right]

(102)

where
\bar{d}=\max_{j\in[n]}\{\|\bm{a}_{j}\|_{2}\}
and
\gamma=\max_{i\in[m],j\in[n]:a_{j,i}>0}\frac{u_{j}}{a_{j,i}}
.

We then consider the setting where
\bm{c}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}
does not hold and only
\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}
is well-defined in (
32
). Still, when
\tilde{r}_{t}\leq\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}
, we must have
\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}=\tilde{x}^{\text{round}}_{t}=0
, which implies
G_{\bm{c}}(I)=0
. We now assume that
\tilde{r}_{t}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}
. Then, since

-\phi\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}\leq\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}-\phi\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}(I_{t+1})-\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c}}(I_{t+1})\leq-\phi\cdot\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}

for any
\phi\in[0,1]
, we have

G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})\leq\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}\cdot(\tilde{r}_{t}-\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})\leq\tilde{r}_{t}-\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}

by noting
\tilde{x}^{*}_{t}\in[0,1]
. Denote by
j_{t}
as the type of query
t
. It holds that

\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{r}_{t}\sim F(\cdot|\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t})}[G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})]\leq\frac{1}{2}\cdot(u_{j_{t}}-\bm{a}_{j_{t}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}\leq(u_{j_{t}}-\bm{a}_{j_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}+(\bm{a}_{j_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{j_{t}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}\leq u_{j_{t}}\cdot(u_{j_{t}}-\bm{a}_{j_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})+(\bm{a}_{j_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{j_{t}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}.

It is clear to see that
\min_{\mu\in\Omega}L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\mu)
is the dual problem of
\max_{\bm{x}}\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{t+1,\bm{c}}
. Denote by
\bar{\bm{x}}=\{\bar{x}_{j}(r)\}\in\text{argmax}_{\bm{x}}\bar{V}^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{t+1,\bm{c}}
such that
\bar{\bm{x}}
and
\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}
is an optimal primal-dual pair.
Then, when
c_{i}<a_{j_{t},i}
for some
i\in[m]
, we must have

\mathbb{E}_{r\sim F(\cdot|\bm{a}_{j_{t}})}[\bar{x}_{j_{t}}(r)]\leq\frac{1}{p_{j_{t}}\cdot(s-1)}

and as a result we have

\alpha\cdot(u_{j_{t}}-\bm{a}_{j_{t}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1})\leq P(\bm{a}_{j_{t}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}\leq r\leq u_{j_{t}}|r\sim F_{j_{t}})=\mathbb{E}_{r\sim F(\cdot|\bm{a}_{j_{t}})}[\bar{x}_{j_{t}}(r)]\leq\frac{1}{p_{j_{t}}\cdot(s-1)}

where
\alpha>0
is a lower bound on the density function specified in
Section

3
. Then we have

\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{r}_{t}\sim F(\cdot|\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t})}[G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})]\right]\leq\frac{u_{j_{t}}}{\alpha\cdot p_{j_{t}}\cdot(s-1)}+\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}[(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}].

Therefore, we have

\mathbb{E}_{I_{t}}[G_{\bm{c}}(I_{t})]\leq\frac{u_{j_{t}}}{\alpha\cdot p_{j_{t}}\cdot(s-1)}+\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{I_{t+1}}[(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{t}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}]\right].

(103)

Our proof is completed by combining (
102
) and (
103
).

\square

Proof of
Lemma

4.9
.

From (
35
),
\mathcal{S}
refers to the space spanned by
\mathcal{A}
, which is a subspace of
\mathbb{R}^{m}
. We further denote by
\mathcal{S}^{\bot}
the orthogonal complement subspace of
S
in the Euclidean space
\mathbb{R}^{m}
, i.e.,
\mathbb{R}^{m}=\mathcal{S}+\mathcal{S}^{\bot}
and
\bm{u}^{\top}\bm{v}=0
for any
\bm{u}\in\mathcal{S}
and any
\bm{v}\in\mathcal{S}^{\bot}
.

Following basics in linear algebra, any vector
\bm{\mu}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
can be decomposed uniquely as
\bm{\mu}=\bm{\mu}_{S}+\bm{\mu}_{S^{\bot}}
, where
\bm{\mu}_{S}\in\mathcal{S}
and
\bm{\mu}_{S^{\bot}}\in\mathcal{S}^{\bot}
. Then the projection of
\bm{\mu}
to the subspace
\mathcal{S}
can be given as
\mathcal{P}_{S}(\bm{\mu})=\bm{\mu}_{S}
. Following this decomposition, for any
\bm{c}\geq 0
, we have that

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{\mu}\geq\Omega}L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu})

\displaystyle=\min_{\bm{\mu}_{S}+\bm{\mu}_{S^{\bot}}\in\Omega}\left(\frac{\bm{c}}{T-t}\right)^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{S}+\left(\frac{\bm{c}}{T-t}\right)^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{S^{\bot}}+\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{S}]^{+}

\displaystyle=\min_{\bm{\mu}_{S}\in\mathcal{S}}\left(\frac{\bm{c}}{T-t}\right)^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{S}+h(\bm{\mu}_{S})+\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{S}]^{+}

where we note that
\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{S^{\bot}}=0
for any
\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}
and

h(\bm{\mu}_{S})=\min_{\bm{\mu}_{S^{\bot}}\in S^{\bot}}\left(\frac{\bm{c}}{T-t}\right)^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{S^{\bot}}\text{\penalty\ s.t.\penalty\ }\bm{\mu}_{S^{\bot}}+\bm{\mu}_{S}\in\Omega.

Note that if the optimization problem that defines
h(\bm{\mu}_{S})
is infeasible for some
\bm{\mu}_{S}
, then we simply set
h(\bm{\mu}_{S})=+\infty
. Denote by
\hat{\mathcal{S}}\subset\mathcal{S}\cap\Omega
the convex set such that
h(\bm{\mu})
is finite for all
\bm{\mu}\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}
. Therefore, the dual problem
\min_{\bm{\mu}\in\Omega}L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu})
can be transferred into a minimization problem over the set
\hat{\mathcal{S}}
, i.e.,

\min_{\bm{\mu}\in\Omega}L^{\mathrm{Fld}}_{\bm{c},t+1}(\bm{\mu})=\min_{\bm{\mu}\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}}\hat{G}_{\bm{c}}(\bm{\mu}):=\left(\frac{\bm{c}}{T-t}\right)^{\top}\bm{\mu}+h(\bm{\mu})+\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}]^{+}.

Following the same way, the minimization of the sample average dual function
L^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c},I_{t+1}}(\bm{\mu})
in (
31
) can be formulated as

\min_{\bm{\mu}\in\Omega}L^{\mathrm{Off}}_{\bm{c},I_{t+1}}(\bm{\mu})=\min_{\bm{\mu}\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}}\hat{G}_{\bm{c},I_{t+1}}(\bm{\mu}):=\left(\frac{\bm{c}}{T-t}\right)^{\top}\bm{\mu}+h(\bm{\mu})+\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}[\tilde{r}_{\tau}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{\tau}\bm{\mu}]^{+}.

We denote by

\bm{\mu}^{*}(\bm{c}^{\prime})\in\argmin_{\mu\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}}\hat{G}_{\bm{c}^{\prime}}(\bm{\mu})\text{\penalty\ and\penalty\ }\tilde{\bm{\mu}}(\bm{c}^{\prime})\in\argmin_{\bm{\mu}\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}}\hat{G}_{\bm{c}^{\prime},I_{t+1}}(\bm{\mu})

(104)

for any
\bm{c}^{\prime}\geq 0
,
where
\tilde{\bm{\mu}}(\bm{c}^{\prime})
is a random variable whose value depends on
I_{t+1}
. Clearly, we have that

\mu^{*}(\bm{c})=\mathcal{P}_{S}(\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1}),\penalty\ \text{and}\penalty\ \tilde{\bm{\mu}}(\bm{c})=\mathcal{P}_{S}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}),\text{\penalty\ and\penalty\ }\tilde{\bm{\mu}}(\bm{c}-\bm{a}_{t})=\mathcal{P}_{S}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}).

(105)

Thus, in order to bound
\mathbb{E}[(\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{1}-\bm{a}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1})^{2}]
, it is sufficient to bound

\mathbb{E}[(\bm{\mu}^{*}(\bm{c}^{\prime})-\tilde{\bm{\mu}}(\bm{c}^{\prime}))^{2}]

(106)

for any
\bm{c}^{\prime}\geq 0
, where the
\mathbb{E}[\cdot]
is taken over
I_{t+1}
. In what follows, we will consider bounding (
106
). Since we will assume that
\bm{c}^{\prime}
is now fixed, for notation simplicity, we will drop
\bm{c}^{\prime}
in the expression of
\bm{\mu}^{*}(\bm{c}^{\prime})
,
\tilde{\bm{\mu}}(\bm{c}^{\prime})
,
\hat{G}_{\bm{c}^{\prime}}(\bm{\mu})
and
\hat{G}_{\bm{c}^{\prime},I_{t+1}}(\bm{\mu})
. We simply denote
\bm{\mu}^{*}
,
\tilde{\bm{\mu}}
,
\hat{G}(\bm{\mu})
and
\hat{G}_{I_{t+1}}(\bm{\mu})
.

We first note that the function
h(\bm{\mu})
is a convex function over
\bm{\mu}\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}
. We also note that the function
\hat{G}
is simply a re-formulation of the function
\hat{L}
after projecting the decision variable onto the subspace
\mathcal{S}
. Thus,
Lemma

4.6
implies the following second-order growth condition for the function
\hat{G}
, i.e.,

\hat{G}(\bm{\mu})-\hat{G}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\geq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{2}\cdot\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\text{\penalty\ for\penalty\ any\penalty\ }\bm{\mu}\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}\subset\mathcal{S}

(107)

which implies that
\bm{\mu}^{*}
is unique. Thus, from Lemma 2.1 in
Shapiro 1992
, we know that

\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\frac{2}{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\cdot\sup_{\bm{\mu}\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}\cap B(\bm{\mu}^{*},r_{0})}\left\{\frac{\hat{G}_{I_{t+1}}(\bm{\mu})-\hat{G}(\bm{\mu})-\hat{G}_{I_{t+1}}(\bm{\mu}^{*})+\hat{G}(\bm{\mu}^{*})}{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}\right\}

where
r_{0}=\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}
and
B(\bm{\mu}^{*},r_{0})=\{\bm{\mu}:\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq r_{0}\}
. Therefore, in order to further bound
\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}
, it is sufficient to bound the term

\sup_{\bm{\mu}\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}\cap B(\bm{\mu}^{*},r_{0})}\left\{\frac{\frac{1}{T-t}\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}[\tilde{r}_{\tau}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{\tau}\bm{\mu}]^{+}-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}]^{+}-\frac{1}{T-t}\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}[\tilde{r}_{\tau}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{\tau}\bm{\mu}^{*}]^{+}+\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}]^{+}}{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}\right\}.

We denote by the function

g(\bm{\mu},(r,\bm{a})):=[r-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}]^{+}+\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}.

Moreover, we denote by

\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})=\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}g(\bm{\mu},(\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}))-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g(\bm{\mu},(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}))]

(108)

which is a random variable that depends on
I_{t+1}
.
Then, the above upper bound over
\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}
can be re-formulated as

\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\frac{2}{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\cdot\sup_{\bm{\mu}\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}\cap B(\bm{\mu}^{*},r_{0})}\left\{\frac{\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})-\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})}{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}\right\}.

We denote by
\tilde{E}
the set of
\bm{\mu}
such that
\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})
is non-differentiable. For simplicity, we denote by
\hat{B}(\bm{\mu}^{*},r_{0})=\hat{\mathcal{S}}\cap B(\bm{\mu}^{*},r_{0})
. Note that
\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})
is Liptschitz continuous. Then, from the mean value theorem for Liptschitz function (
Clarke 1990
p41), we have that

\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{4}{\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2}}\cdot\sup_{\bm{\mu}\in\hat{B}(\bm{\mu}^{*},r_{0})\setminus E}\{\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})\|_{2}^{2}\}=\frac{4}{\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2}}\cdot(\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}^{2}+\sup_{\bm{\mu}\in\hat{B}(\bm{\mu}^{*},r_{0})\setminus\tilde{E}}\{\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})-\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}^{2}\}).

(109)

We have the following claim over the term
\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}^{2}
, which is proved at the end of this proof.

Claim 5

It holds that

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}^{2}]\leq\frac{\bar{d}^{2}}{T-t}

where the expectation
\mathbb{E}[\cdot]
takes over
I_{t+1}=\{(\tilde{r}_{t+1},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{t+1}),\dots,(\tilde{r}_{T},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{T})\}
that defines
\tilde{\delta}(\cdot)
in (
108
).

Regarding the term
\sup_{\bm{\mu}\in\hat{B}(\bm{\mu}^{*},r_{0})\setminus\tilde{E}}\{\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})-\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}^{2}\}
, following the steps in
Shapiro 1993
, we can show that

\sup_{\bm{\mu}\in B(\mu^{*},r_{0})\setminus\tilde{E}}\{\frac{\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})-\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T-t}}+r_{0}}\}\rightarrow 0\text{\penalty\ in\penalty\ probability\penalty\ as\penalty\ }T-t\rightarrow\infty.

As a result, there exists a constant
t_{0}
such that as long as
T-t\geq t_{0}
, we have that

\sup_{\bm{\mu}\in\hat{B}(\bm{\mu}^{*},r_{0})\setminus\tilde{E}}\{\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})-\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}^{2}\}\leq\frac{1}{2}\cdot(\frac{1}{T-t}+r_{0}^{2})

with a high probability. We formalize the above step in the following claim for completeness, by making explicit the constant term
t_{0}
, as well as the “high probability”.

Claim 6

It holds that

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq r_{0}}\frac{\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})-\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T-t}}+3\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot r_{0}}\geq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)\leq

\displaystyle 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{3}\left(\frac{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\right)^{2}(T-t)^{-\frac{13}{25}}

(110)

\displaystyle+2\exp\left(-\delta\cdot(T-t)^{12/25}\right)\left(\frac{13(\log(T-t)+\log 2\gamma\sqrt{m})}{25|\log(1-q)|}+1\right)(2M)^{m}

as long as
T-t\geq\hat{t}
, where
q=1/M=\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}/(144\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{2})
, and
\hat{t},\delta
are two constants that is independent of
m
and are determined polynomially by the problem parameters
\underline{\alpha},\underline{\beta},\bar{\alpha},\bar{d}
.

The proof of
6
is relegated to the end of this proof. Denote by

\tilde{z}=\sup_{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq r_{0}}\frac{\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu})-\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T-t}}+3\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot r_{0}}.

We also denote by three events

\mathcal{E}_{1}=\{\tilde{z}\leq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}\}\text{\penalty\ \penalty\ and\penalty\ \penalty\ }\mathcal{E}_{2}=\{\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}<\tilde{z}\text{\penalty\ and\penalty\ }r_{0}\leq\frac{1}{(T-t)^{\frac{7}{25}}}\}\penalty\ \text{\penalty\ \penalty\ and\penalty\ \penalty\ }\mathcal{E}_{3}=\{\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}<\tilde{z}\text{\penalty\ and\penalty\ }r_{0}>\frac{1}{(T-t)^{\frac{7}{25}}}\}.

Clearly, we have

\mathbb{E}[r_{0}^{2}]=P(\mathcal{E}_{1})\cdot\mathbb{E}[r_{0}^{2}|\mathcal{E}_{1}]+P(\mathcal{E}_{2})\cdot\mathbb{E}[r_{0}^{2}|\mathcal{E}_{2}]+P(\mathcal{E}_{2})\cdot\mathbb{E}[r_{0}^{2}|\mathcal{E}_{2}].

(111)

Now, from (
109
),
5
, and the definition of the event
\mathcal{E}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle P(\mathcal{E}_{1})\cdot\mathbb{E}[r_{0}^{2}|\mathcal{E}_{1}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2}}\cdot P(\mathcal{E}_{1})\cdot\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}^{2}|\mathcal{E}_{1}]+P(\mathcal{E}_{1})\cdot\frac{1}{36\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{4}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T-t}}+3\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot r_{0}\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{E}_{1}\right]

(112)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}^{2}]+\frac{1}{2}\cdot P(\mathcal{E}_{1})\cdot\mathbb{E}[r_{0}^{2}|\mathcal{E}_{1}]+\frac{1}{18\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{4}\cdot(T-t)}

which implies that

P(\mathcal{E}_{1})\cdot\mathbb{E}[r_{0}^{2}|\mathcal{E}_{1}]\leq\frac{8\bar{d}^{2}}{\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2}\cdot(T-t)}+\frac{1}{9\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot(T-t)}.

(113)

For the term
P(\mathcal{E}_{2})\cdot\mathbb{E}[r_{0}^{2}|\mathcal{E}_{2}]
, from the definition of the event
\mathcal{E}_{2}
and
6
, we have

\displaystyle P(\mathcal{E}_{2})\cdot\mathbb{E}[r_{0}^{2}|\mathcal{E}_{2}]\leq P(\mathcal{E}_{2})\cdot\frac{1}{(T-t)^{\frac{14}{25}}}\leq

\displaystyle 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{3}\left(\frac{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\right)^{2}(T-t)^{-\frac{27}{25}}

(114)

\displaystyle+2\exp\left(-\delta\cdot(T-t)^{12/25}\right)\left(\frac{13(\log(T-t)+\log 2\gamma\sqrt{m})}{25|\log(1-q)|}+1\right)(2M)^{m}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T-t}

as long as
T-t>t_{1}
, where

t_{1}=O\left(\left(mM\cdot\frac{\bar{\alpha}^{3}\bar{d}^{7}}{\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2}}\right)^{\frac{25}{2}}\right).

We now want to bound the probability that event
\mathcal{E}_{3}
happens. Note that we have

\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{2}\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T-t}\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}[\tilde{r}_{\tau}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{\tau}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}]^{+}-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}]^{+}-\frac{1}{T-t}\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}[\tilde{r}_{\tau}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{\tau}\bm{\mu}^{*}]^{+}+\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}]^{+}.

(115)

By modifying the approach in
Li and Ye 2021
, we have the following lemma, which follows Proposition 2 and Proposition 3 of
Li and Ye 2021
and is proved at the end of this proof.

Lemma F.1

It holds that

P\left(\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\geq k_{1}/(\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2})\cdot\varepsilon^{2}\right)\leq k_{2}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{q}\right)^{m}\cdot\log\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{k_{3}\varepsilon^{2}(T-t)}{\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

where
k_{1}
,
k_{2}
,
k_{3}
are constant numbers independent of problem parameters and
q
is a constant such that
\bar{\alpha}\cdot\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\frac{q(1+q)}{(1-q)^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{32}
.

By setting
\epsilon=\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{k_{1}\cdot(T-t)^{\frac{7}{25}}}
in
Lemma

F.1
, we have that

P(\mathcal{E}_{3})\leq P\left(\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|^{2}_{2}\geq\left(\frac{1}{(T-t)^{\frac{7}{25}}}\right)^{2}\right)\leq O\left((2/q)^{m}\right)\cdot\log(T-t)\cdot\exp(-(T-t)^{\frac{11}{25}})\leq\frac{1}{T-t}

(116)

as long as
T-t>t_{2}
where
t_{2}=O(\frac{m}{q})
.Therefore, combining (
111
), (
113
), (
114
), and (
116
), we have

\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}]=\mathbb{E}[r_{0}^{2}]\leq\frac{8\bar{d}^{2}}{\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2}\cdot(T-t)}+\frac{1}{9\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot(T-t)}+\frac{2}{T-t}

as long as
T-t>t_{0}
, where

t_{0}=O\left(\left(mM\cdot\frac{\bar{\alpha}^{3}\bar{d}^{7}}{\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2}}\right)^{\frac{25}{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{q}\right).

Therefore, our proof is completed.

\square

Proof of
5
.

Note that

\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})=-\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{\tau}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}\}}+\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[\tilde{\bm{a}}\cdot(1-F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}|\tilde{\bm{a}}))].

Clearly, for each
\tau=t+1,\ldots,T
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau})}[\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{\tau}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}\}}]=\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[\tilde{\bm{a}}\cdot(1-F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}|\tilde{\bm{a}}))].

Thus, we have

\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\tilde{\delta}(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}^{2}]=\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\tilde{r}_{\tau}\geq\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}\}})\leq\frac{\bar{d}^{2}}{T-t}

which completes our proof.

\square

Proof of
6
.

We denote by

\psi((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu})=\nabla g(\bm{\mu},(r,\bm{a}))-\nabla g(\bm{\mu}^{*},(r,\bm{a})).

We also denote by

\lambda(\bm{\mu})=\mathbb{E}_{(r,\bm{a})}[\psi((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu})]

and

u((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu},d)=\sup_{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq d}\|\psi((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu})-\psi((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}.

Clearly, we have that

\lambda(\bm{\mu}^{*})=0.

(117)

Thus, condition (N-2) in
Huber 1967
is satisfied. Also, for any
\bm{\mu}
, from definition of the functions
\psi
and
\lambda
, we have that

\|\lambda(\bm{\mu})\|_{2}=\|2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}(\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*})+\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[\tilde{\bm{a}}\cdot(F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}|\tilde{\bm{a}})-F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}|\tilde{\bm{a}}))]\|_{2}.

Note that

F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}|\tilde{\bm{a}})-F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}|\tilde{\bm{a}})\leq\bar{\alpha}\cdot|\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}|\leq\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}\cdot\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\lambda(\bm{\mu})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}-\|\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[\tilde{\bm{a}}\cdot(F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}|\tilde{\bm{a}})-F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}|\tilde{\bm{a}}))]\|_{2}

(118)

\displaystyle\geq 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}-\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}=\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}

for any
d_{0}\geq 0
and
\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq d_{0}
.
Thus, we verify that condition (N-3) (i) in
Huber 1967
holds. Moreover, we have

\|\psi((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu})-\psi((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}=\|\bm{a}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{r\geq\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}\}}-\bm{a}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{r\geq\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}\}}\|\leq\|\bm{a}\cdot(\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}\leq r\leq\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}\leq r\leq\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}\}})\|\leq\bar{d}

(119)

which implies that

\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[u((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\mu},d)]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[\|\tilde{\bm{a}}\|_{2}\cdot 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}d]\leq 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot d.

(120)

Similarly, we have

\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[u((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\mu},d)^{2}]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[\|\tilde{\bm{a}}\|_{2}^{2}\cdot 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}d]\leq 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{3}\cdot d.

Thus, we verify conditions (N-3) (ii) and (iii) in
Huber 1967
for any
d_{0}\geq 0
. Clearly,
\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\psi((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\mu}^{*})]
is finite, which verifies condition (N-4) in
Huber 1967
. Thus, denote by

Z_{T-t}(\bm{\mu},\bm{\mu}^{*})=\frac{\|\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}(\psi((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\mu})-\psi((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\mu}^{*}))-\lambda(\bm{\mu})+\lambda(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T-t}}+\|\lambda(\bm{\mu})\|_{2}}.

By
Lemma

D.5
, for any
\epsilon>0
and any
\gamma^{\prime}\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
, we have

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|\leq d_{0}}Z_{T-t}(\bm{\mu},\bm{\mu}^{*})\geq 2\epsilon\right)\leq 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{3}\epsilon^{-2}(T-t)^{-\gamma^{\prime}}

(121)

\displaystyle+2\exp\left(-\frac{\min\{4\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{4}q,\varepsilon^{2}\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{4}\}\cdot(1-q)^{2}n^{1-\gamma^{\prime}}}{2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}(3-q)+2\bar{d}(2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}+\varepsilon\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{3})(1-q)/3}\right)\left(\frac{\gamma^{\prime}(\log(T-t)+\log d_{0})}{|\log(1-q)|}+1\right)(2M)^{m}

as long as
T-t\geq n_{0}
, where
n_{0}
satisfying
n_{0}^{\gamma^{\prime}-\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{4\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}{\epsilon}
and
\gamma^{\prime}\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
is an arbitrary number. Moreover, we set
M\geq(6\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{2})/(\epsilon\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2})=6\bar{\alpha}/\epsilon
and
q=1/M
.

We first specify
\epsilon=\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{24\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}
and
\gamma^{\prime}=13/25
. Then, (
121
) will become

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|\leq d_{0}}Z_{T-t}(\bm{\mu},\bm{\mu}^{*})\geq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)\leq 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{3}\left(\frac{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\right)^{2}(T-t)^{-\frac{13}{25}}

(122)

\displaystyle+2\exp\left(-\delta\cdot(T-t)^{12/25}\right)\left(\frac{13(\log(T-t)+\log d_{0})}{25|\log(1-q)|}+1\right)(2M)^{m}

as long as
T-t\geq(96\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{4}/(\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}))^{50}
,
M=144\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{2}/(\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta})
,
q=1/M=\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}/(144\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{2})
, and

\delta=\frac{\min\{4\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{4}q,\varepsilon^{2}\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{4}\}\cdot(1-q)^{2}}{2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}(3-q)+2\bar{d}(2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}+\varepsilon\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{3})(1-q)/3}

(123)

with
M=144\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{2}/(\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta})
and
q=1/M=\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}/(144\bar{\alpha}^{2}\bar{d}^{2})
. Note that

\nabla\delta(\bm{\mu})-\nabla\delta(\bm{\mu}^{*})=\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}(\psi((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\mu})-\psi((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\mu}^{*}))-\lambda(\bm{\mu})+\lambda(\bm{\mu}^{*}).

We know that (
122
) implies that

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|\leq d_{0}}\frac{\|\nabla\delta(\bm{\mu})-\nabla\delta(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T-t}}+\|\lambda(\bm{\mu})\|_{2}}\geq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)\leq

\displaystyle 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{3}\left(\frac{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\right)^{2}(T-t)^{-\frac{13}{25}}

(124)

\displaystyle+2\exp\left(-\delta\cdot(T-t)^{12/25}\right)\left(\frac{13(\log(T-t)+\log d_{0})}{25|\log(1-q)|}+1\right)(2M)^{m}.

From
Section

3
, we have
\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}\|_{2}\leq\gamma\sqrt{m}
and
\|\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\gamma\sqrt{m}
, which implies that
r_{0}=\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq 2\gamma\sqrt{m}
. Therefore, we can simply set
d_{0}=2\gamma\sqrt{m}
and we know that
r_{0}
is always upper bounded by
d_{0}
, which implies that

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|\leq r_{0}}\frac{\|\nabla\delta(\bm{\mu})-\nabla\delta(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T-t}}+\|\lambda(\bm{\mu})\|_{2}}\geq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|\leq d_{0}}\frac{\|\nabla\delta(\bm{\mu})-\nabla\delta(\bm{\mu}^{*})\|_{2}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{T-t}}+\|\lambda(\bm{\mu})\|_{2}}\geq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

(125)

\displaystyle\leq 2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{3}\left(\frac{12\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}}{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}\right)^{2}(T-t)^{-\frac{13}{25}}

\displaystyle+2\exp\left(-\delta\cdot(T-t)^{12/25}\right)\left(\frac{13(\log(T-t)+\log 2\gamma\sqrt{m})}{25|\log(1-q)|}+1\right)(2M)^{m}

where the first inequality follows from
r_{0}\leq d_{0}=2\gamma\sqrt{m}
almost surely and the second inequality follows from (
124
) by setting
d_{0}=2\gamma\sqrt{m}
. Therefore, our proof of (
110
) is completed by noting that

\|\lambda(\bm{\mu})\|_{2}=\|2\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}(\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*})+\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}[\tilde{\bm{a}}\cdot(F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}|\tilde{\bm{a}})-F(\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}|\tilde{\bm{a}}))]\|_{2}\leq 3\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}.

\square

Proof of
Lemma

F.1
.

Following
Li and Ye 2021
, we denote by

\phi((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu})=-\bm{a}^{\top}\cdot\mathbbm{1}(r>\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}).

Then, from Lemma 1 of
Li and Ye 2021
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}]^{+}-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\tilde{r}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}]^{+}=\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\phi((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\mu}^{*})]\cdot(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*})+\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}\left[\int_{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}^{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}}(\mathbbm{1}(\tilde{r}>v)-\mathbbm{1}(\tilde{r}>\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}))dv\right].

(126)

Also, from Lemma 2 of
Li and Ye 2021
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}([\tilde{r}_{\tau}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{\tau}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}]^{+}-[\tilde{r}_{\tau}-\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}_{\tau}\bm{\mu}^{*}]^{+})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\phi((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\mu}^{*})\cdot(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*})

(127)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\int_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}^{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}}(\mathbbm{1}(\tilde{r}_{\tau}>v)-\mathbbm{1}(\tilde{r}_{\tau}>\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}))dv.

Plugging (
126
) and (
127
) into (
115
), we have that

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{2}\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\phi((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\mu}^{*})\cdot(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\phi((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\mu}^{*})]\cdot(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*})}_{I}

(128)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\int_{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}^{\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}}(\mathbbm{1}(\tilde{r}_{\tau}>v)-\mathbbm{1}(\tilde{r}_{\tau}>\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}))dv-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}\left[\int_{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}}^{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}}(\mathbbm{1}(\tilde{r}>v)-\mathbbm{1}(\tilde{r}>\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}))dv\right]}_{II}.

We proceed by bounding the term I and term II separately.

Bound I:
by Matrix Hoeffding’s inequality, we have that

P\left(\|\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\phi((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\mu}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\phi((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\mu}^{*})]\|_{2}\geq\epsilon\right)\leq m\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\cdot(T-t)}{\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

which implies that

P\left(\left|I\right|\geq\epsilon\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\right)\leq m\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\cdot(T-t)}{\bar{d}^{2}}\right).

(129)

Bound II:

We define a function

\eta((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu}_{1},\bm{\mu}_{2})=\int_{\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{1}}^{\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{2}}(\mathbbm{1}(r>v)-\mathbbm{1}(r>\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}))dv

for any
\bm{\mu}_{1},\bm{\mu}_{2}\in\hat{\mathcal{S}}
. We have

II=\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\mu}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\mu}^{*})].

(130)

We utilize a splitting scheme to split the set
\{\bm{\mu}:\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{m}\cdot\gamma\}
into disjoint cubes, similar to
Huber 1967
and
Li and Ye 2021
. It is clear to see that
\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{m}\cdot\gamma
. The idea is to divide the region
\{\bm{\mu}:\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{m}\cdot\gamma\}
into a slowly increasing number of smaller cubes. We consider the concentric cubes:

C_{k}=\{\bm{\mu}:\|\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq(1-q)^{k}\cdot\sqrt{m}\gamma\},\penalty\ \penalty\ k=1,\dots,k_{0}

where
q
is a constant such that
\bar{\alpha}\cdot\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\frac{q(1+q)}{(1-q)^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{32}
, and
k_{0}
is a constants such that
(1-q)^{k_{0}}\cdot\sqrt{m}\gamma=\epsilon
. We then further divide the region
C_{k-1}\setminus C_{k}
into cubes
\{\Omega_{kl}\}_{l=1}^{l_{k}}
with edges of length
2(1-q)^{k}\cdot q\cdot\sqrt{m}\gamma
such that the centers of these cubes
\bm{\xi}_{kl}
satisfies

\|\bm{\xi}_{kl}\|_{2}=(1-q)^{k-1}(1-\frac{q}{2})\cdot\sqrt{m}\gamma,\penalty\ \penalty\ l=1,\dots,k_{l}.

In total, there are no more than
k_{0}\cdot(\frac{2}{q})^{m}
number of cubes. We now denote by
\tilde{\Omega}
the cube that contains
\tilde{\bm{\mu}}
and denote by
\tilde{\bm{\xi}}
the center of the cube
\tilde{\Omega}
. Note that both
\tilde{\Omega}
and
\tilde{\bm{\xi}}
are random, where the randomness arises from
\tilde{\bm{\mu}}
.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\mu}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\mu}^{*})]=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\tilde{\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\tilde{\bm{\xi}})]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\bm{\mu}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\bm{\mu}^{*})]

which implies that

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\mu}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\mu}^{*})]\geq\epsilon^{2}+\epsilon\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{8}\right)

(131)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\tilde{\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\tilde{\bm{\xi}})]\geq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{2}+\frac{\epsilon\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{16}\right)

\displaystyle+P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\bm{\mu}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\bm{\mu}^{*})]\geq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{2}+\frac{\epsilon\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{16}\right).

We have the following result.

Claim 7

It holds that

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\tilde{\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\tilde{\bm{\xi}})]\geq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{2}+\frac{\epsilon\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}\|_{2}^{2}}{16}\right)

\displaystyle\leq k_{0}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{q}\right)^{m}\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}(T-t)}{2q^{2}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

where
q
is a constant such that
\bar{\alpha}\cdot\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\frac{q(1+q)}{(1-q)^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{32}
, and
k_{0}
is a constants such that
(1-q)^{k_{0}}\cdot\sqrt{m}\gamma=\epsilon
.

We also have the following result.

Claim 8

It holds that

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\bm{\mu}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\bm{\mu}^{*})]\geq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{2}+\frac{\epsilon\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{16}\right)

\displaystyle\leq k_{0}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{q}\right)^{m}\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\cdot(T-t)q^{2}}{2\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

where
q
is a constant such that
\bar{\alpha}\cdot\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\frac{q(1+q)}{(1-q)^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{32}
, and
k_{0}
is a constants such that
(1-q)^{k_{0}}\cdot\sqrt{m}\gamma=\epsilon
.

Combining
7
,
8
and (
131
), we have that

\displaystyle P\left(\text{II}\geq\epsilon^{2}+\epsilon\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{8}\right)

(132)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\mu}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\mu}^{*})]\geq\epsilon^{2}+\epsilon\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{8}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle k_{0}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{q}\right)^{m}\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}(T-t)}{2q^{2}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)+k_{0}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{q}\right)^{m}\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\cdot(T-t)q^{2}}{2\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

where
q
is a constant such that
\bar{\alpha}\cdot\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\frac{q(1+q)}{(1-q)^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{32}
, and
k_{0}
is a constants such that
(1-q)^{k_{0}}\cdot\sqrt{m}\gamma=\epsilon
. Our bound of the term II is now completed.

We now use the bound on I and II to prove the probability bound on the event
\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\geq\epsilon^{2}
. Combining (
129
) and (
132
), we have

\displaystyle P\left(\text{I}+\text{II}\geq 2\epsilon^{2}+2\epsilon\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{4}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle P\left(\text{I}\geq\epsilon^{2}+\epsilon\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{8}\right)+P\left(\text{II}\geq\epsilon^{2}+\epsilon\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{8}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle m\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\cdot(T-t)}{\bar{d}^{2}}\right)+k_{0}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{q}\right)^{m}\cdot\left(\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}(T-t)}{2q^{2}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\cdot(T-t)q^{2}}{2\bar{d}^{2}}\right)\right)

where
c_{2}
is a constant. Further note that

\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{2}\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\text{I}+\text{II},

and

\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{2}\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\geq 2\epsilon^{2}+2\epsilon\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{4}

implies that

\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\geq\epsilon^{2}\cdot\frac{4(2+\sqrt{2\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}+4})^{2}}{\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2}}.

Therefore, we have that

\displaystyle P\left(\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\geq\epsilon^{2}\cdot\frac{4(2+\sqrt{2\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}+4})^{2}}{\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2}}\right)\leq

\displaystyle m\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\cdot(T-t)}{\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+k_{0}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{q}\right)^{m}\cdot\left(\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}(T-t)}{2q^{2}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)+\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\cdot(T-t)q^{2}}{2\bar{d}^{2}}\right)\right).

By noting that
k_{0}=O(\log(\frac{1}{\varepsilon})
, we have

P\left(\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\geq k_{1}/(\underline{\alpha}^{2}\underline{\beta}^{2})\cdot\varepsilon^{2}\right)\leq k_{2}\cdot\left(\frac{2}{q}\right)^{m}\cdot\log\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{k_{3}\varepsilon^{2}(T-t)}{\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

where
k_{1}
,
k_{2}
,
k_{3}
are constant numbers and
q
is a constant such that
\bar{\alpha}\cdot\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\frac{q(1+q)}{(1-q)^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{32}
. Our proof is thus completed.

\square

Proof of
7
.

We denote by

g((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu}_{1},d)=\sup_{\bm{\mu}_{2}:\|\bm{\mu}_{2}-\bm{\mu}_{1}\|_{2}\leq d}\eta((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\mu}_{1},\bm{\mu}_{2}).

Then we have

\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\tilde{\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\tilde{\bm{\xi}})]\leq\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}g((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})+\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})]

where
\tilde{d}
denotes the edge length of the cube
\tilde{\Omega}
. We now consider the cubes
\{\Omega_{kl}\}
and the center concentric cubes
C_{k_{0}}
separately.

(i). If the cube
\tilde{\Omega}\in\{\Omega_{kl}\}
, then, we note that

\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}\left[\sup_{\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}:\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{\xi}}\|_{2}\leq\tilde{d}}\int^{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}}\int_{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\mu}}_{2}}^{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\xi}}}(\mathbbm{1}(r>v)-\mathbbm{1}(r>\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}))dvdF(r|\tilde{\bm{a}})\right]\leq\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\tilde{d}\cdot(\|\bm{\mu}^{*}-\tilde{\bm{\xi}}\|_{2}+\tilde{d}).

By definition, we have
\tilde{d}=q\cdot\|\bm{\mu}^{*}-\tilde{\bm{\xi}}\|_{2}
and
\|\bm{\mu}^{*}-\tilde{\bm{\mu}}\|_{2}\geq\|\bm{\mu}^{*}-\tilde{\bm{\xi}}\|_{2}-\tilde{d}
. Then, we have

\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})]\leq\bar{\alpha}\cdot\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\frac{q(1+q)}{(1-q)^{2}}\cdot\|\bm{\mu}^{*}-\tilde{\bm{\mu}}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{32}

by specifying
q
such that
\bar{\alpha}\cdot\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\frac{q(1+q)}{(1-q)^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}}{32}
.

(ii). If the cube
\tilde{\Omega}=C_{k_{0}}
, then by noting
\tilde{\bm{\xi}}=\bm{\mu}^{*}
and
(1-q)^{k_{0}}\cdot\sqrt{m}\gamma=\epsilon
, we have that

\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}}\left[\sup_{\bm{\mu}_{2}:\|\bm{\mu}_{2}-\tilde{\bm{\xi}}\|_{2}\leq\tilde{d}}\int^{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}}_{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{2}}\int_{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}_{2}}^{\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\tilde{\bm{\xi}}}(\mathbbm{1}(r>v)-\mathbbm{1}(r>\tilde{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{\mu}^{*}))dvdF(r|\tilde{\bm{a}})\right]\leq\bar{\alpha}\cdot\bar{d}^{2}\cdot\epsilon^{2}.

Therefore, for both (i) and (ii), it holds that

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\tilde{\bm{\xi}})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\mu}},\tilde{\bm{\xi}})]\geq\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\epsilon^{2}+\frac{\epsilon\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{16}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}g((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})+\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})]\geq\bar{\alpha}\bar{d}^{2}\epsilon^{2}+\frac{\epsilon\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}{2}+\frac{\underline{\alpha}\underline{\beta}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}}{16}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}g((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})]\geq\frac{\epsilon\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}g((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})]\geq\frac{\epsilon\cdot\tilde{d}}{2q}\right).

We apply the Hoeffding inequality to bound the above probability for each cube
\Omega_{kl}
, and
C_{k_{0}}
. It holds that

g((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\xi}_{kl},d_{kl})\leq\bar{d}\cdot d_{kl},\penalty\ \text{and\penalty\ }g((r,\bm{a}),\bm{\xi}_{k_{0}},d_{k_{0}})\leq\bar{d}\cdot d_{k_{0}}\penalty\ \penalty\ \forall(r,\bm{a})

where
d_{kl}
denotes the length of the edge of the cube
\Omega_{kl}
and
d_{k_{0}}
denotes the length of the edge of the cube
C_{k_{0}}
.
By Hoeffding’s inequality, we have

P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}g((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\xi}_{kl},d_{kl})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\xi}_{kl},d_{kl})]\geq\frac{\epsilon\cdot d_{kl}}{2q}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}(T-t)}{2q^{2}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

for each cube
\Omega_{kl}
, and

P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}g((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\xi}_{k_{0}},d_{k_{0}})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\xi}_{k_{0}},d_{k_{0}})]\geq\frac{\epsilon\cdot d_{k_{0}}}{2q}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}(T-t)}{2q^{2}\bar{d}^{2}}\right)

for the cube
C_{k_{0}}
.
Note that

\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}g((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\tilde{d})]\geq\frac{\epsilon\cdot\tilde{d}}{2q}

implies that

\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t}^{T}g((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\xi}_{k_{0}},d_{k_{0}})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\xi}_{k_{0}},d_{k_{0}})]\geq\frac{\epsilon\cdot d_{k_{0}}}{2q},

or there exists at least one
\Omega_{kl}
such that

\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t}^{T}g((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\xi}_{kl},d_{kl})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[g((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\xi}_{kl},d_{kl})]\geq\frac{\epsilon\cdot d_{kl}}{2q}.

Applying the union bound, our proof is completed.

\square

Proof of
8
.

Note that

\eta((r,\bm{a}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\bm{\mu}^{*})\leq\bar{d}\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\xi}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}.

Then, if
\tilde{\Omega}\in\{\Omega_{kl}\}
, we have that

\eta((r,\bm{a}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\bm{\mu}^{*})\leq\bar{d}\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\xi}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}\leq\frac{\bar{d}\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}{q}

and if
\tilde{\Omega}=C_{k_{0}}
, we have that

\eta((r,\bm{a}),\tilde{\bm{\xi}},\bm{\mu}^{*})\leq\bar{d}\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\xi}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}=0

by noting
\tilde{\bm{\xi}}=\bm{\xi}_{k_{0}}=\bm{\mu}^{*}
.
Now, for each
\Omega_{kl}
, by Hoeffding’s inequality, we have

P\left(\frac{1}{T-t}\cdot\sum_{\tau=t+1}^{T}\eta((\tilde{r}_{\tau},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{\tau}),\bm{\xi}_{kl},\bm{\mu}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}})}[\eta((\tilde{r},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{\xi}_{kl},\bm{\mu}^{*})]\geq\frac{\epsilon\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}}{2}\right)\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon^{2}\cdot(T-t)q^{2}}{2\bar{d}^{2}}\right).

Our proof is completed from the union bound over all
\Omega_{kl}
.

\square
